# H02 review summary — 2026-10-26

**Outcome: PASS (HW OBSERVED), OFF-only scope.** Corrected replay used CP2102 USB–TTL `10C4:EA60` on `/dev/ttyUSB3`, 9600 8N1. Operator confirmed the menu firmware as Main 1.04 / Aux 0.01; the raw `*IDN?` revision field was `1.04-1.04` during this run and is retained as reported, not used as a compatibility gate.

- Distinct profile readbacks: CH1 1.00 V / 0.100 A / V-cap 2.00 V / I-cap 0.300 A; CH2 1.50 V / 0.200 A / 3.00 V / 0.400 A; CH3 2.00 V / 0.300 A / 4.00 V / 0.500 A. Outputs and timers read OFF.
- Front-panel V-set attempts 2.01/3.01/4.01 V read back as 2.00/3.00/4.00 V on CH1/2/3; each panel stage had all outputs OFF and a clean error queue.
- Library soft guard rejected above-cap V/I requests before SCPI dispatch. Explicitly approved debug-bypass probes sent 2.01/3.01/4.01 V through the shared driver path. Each instrument response was `120, "Parameter overflowed"`; setpoints remained 1.00/1.50/2.00 V. Error queue returned `0, "No error"` after cleanup.
- 407 recorded queue reads were `0, "No error"`; three expected hard-limit probes produced error 120. Final internal measurements were CH1 0.00369263 V / 0 A, CH2 0.00183105 V / 0 A, CH3 0 V / 0.000290878 A; accuracy was not evaluated. Operator's final gate confirmed all outputs OFF and load OFF/disconnected.
- The first replay stopped before completing panel checks because its operator instruction did not identify the V-set button sequence. That was a runner/card clarity issue, not evidence of a library defect. The corrected replay completed all H02 checks.

This qualifies the requested off-state profile ceiling behavior and software/API guards. It does not test powered regulation, load current, or independent analog OVP. Offline suite evidence is SIM TESTED, separate from this physical result.
